Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the stochastic_analysis space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 416 and the convergence rate is O(31/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the stochastic_analysis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 416 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 31. QED.
